# B1 explicit-copy regeneration, final run: the listed sites (2026-10-23)

The output of `yo fix ./src --migrate explicit-copy --hot-path src/evaluator --hot-path src/lexer.yo --hot-path src/env.yo --std-path ./std` by the tree binary of `e094cc8bc` (develop `617cfc052` with `perf/share-token-input` `b8463d4d5` merged in; its tree is identical to develop `54e94f11e`, where that branch squash-merged as #1561), on that tree. It holds the 1,963 sites the run left as written, followed by its summary line. Positions are the pre-rewrite tree's. The 632 inserted `.clone()` calls are the regeneration commit of branch `feat/v2b-b1-regeneration-2`. The run, its measurements and the verdict are in `plans/VALUES_BY_DEFAULT.md`, B1, "As-built, re-run 2026-10-23" and its "Final regeneration" entry. This listing supersedes `VBD_B1_EXPLICIT_COPY_LISTING_2026-10-22.md` and the re-run's 1,959-site listing (`412258a2a`) as the input for B3 and B4; the sites move when the tree does, so re-run the dry run on the tip before using it.
